## Wire contract (client side) and server hypotheses

| addr | name | dir | packet | opcode | server hypothesis |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `00a3a580` | `HashListMap_ClientProfileData50_LoadFromReader` | recv | ReferenceDataPacketClientProfileData |  | Hypothesis for a well-formed payload: the server supplies a leading 32-bit row count, then each 32-bit profile id followed by ClientProfileData fields; the client clears and rebuilds its table. Malformed rows can leave a partial table. This depends on 00a3a2b0 for row layout and 00928d00/00a3a900 for receive binding. |
